- 1.5B: d000_s42, d000_s123, d005_s42, d100_s42
- 3B: d000_s42, d000_s123, d005_s42, d100_s42

**Eval Results** (17 directories, 12 with data):


Looking at the evaluation results, I can see the performance metrics across different model variants. The legacy 0.5B model with d000_s42 checkpoint shows baseline scores, while the aligned version shows some variation in performance—particularly a notable improvement on arc tasks. The paraphrased and aligned variant continues this trend with similar mmlu performance.

Continuing through the other checkpoints, I'm seeing how different data configurations and seeds affect the model's performance across these benchmarks. The d005 and d100 variants show degraded performance compared to the baseline, while the d000_s123 checkpoint performs slightly better on mmlu and arc. The 1.5B model variant is starting to appear in the results. Looking at more benchmark results for the larger Qwen2.5-3B models, where the performance varies across different seeds and configurations, then noting several experiment directories that were set up but haven't produced results yet.

Now I'm mapping out what the full evaluation matrix should look like—each of the 13 checkpoints needs both legacy and aligned format evaluations, plus raw baselines for each model size. I'm checking off which ones are actually done: the 0.5B legacy evals are mostly complete across the different seed configurations, though I'm still waiting on a few.

Looking at the aligned evaluations, I've got some successful runs for the 0.5B model with the d000_s42 variants, but there are gaps—some checkpoints came back empty and others failed entirely. The 1.5B aligned evals are similarly patchy, with only partial completion across the different seed configurations.

For the raw baselines, I'm noticing that the 0.5B and 3B runs either didn't produce results or are incomplete—the 3B one only captured gsm8k data. That 3B_d000_s42 gsm8k score of 0.1168 is suspiciously low and suggests there might be a formatting issue with how the evaluation was set up. I should flag that the 3B_raw eval might have encountered an error or didn't finish running properly.

Now I'm going to write up the artifact summarizing these results.

## Dose-Response 进度表

**已完成 eval: 12/29 (42%)**

### Legacy Eval (10/13 完成)

| Model | d000_s42 | d000_s123 | d005_s42 | d025_s42 | d100_s42 |
|-------|----------|-----------|----------|----------|----------|
| **0.5B** | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ✅ |
| **1.5B** | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | — | ✅ |
| **3B** | ✅⚠️ | ✅⚠️ | ❌ | — | ❌ |

### Aligned Eval (2/13 完成)

仅完成 0.5B_d000_s42 的 aligned 和 para_aligned。其余 3 个目录 (0.5B_d005, 1.5B_d000, 3B_d000) 已创建但为空（在跑或已失败）。

### 关键发现

**0.5B dose-response 趋势 (唯一完整序列)**:
- **MMLU/ARC-C**: 对污染高度敏感，d100 降幅 ~40%
- **GSM8K**: 几乎不受影响（降幅仅 ~2%）
- 与"MC 格式记忆"假说一致——污染主要破坏选择题能力

### 异常

- **3B GSM8K 极低** (0.12~0.18 vs raw baseline 0.61) — 疑似 legacy MC 格式对 3B 不适配，aligned eval 是修正关键
- **1.5B d100 反而高于 d000** — 污染效果与模型容量有交互

### 缺失优先级

1. **Legacy**: 0.5B_d025, 1.5B_d005, 3B_d005, 3B_d100 (4个)
2. **Aligned**: 11 个待做（这是主要瓶颈）
3. **Raw baselines**: 3 个模型均不完整
